      420 +## 2026-10-29 · Inter Display through Inter's optical size axi

      421 +                                                              
      422 +- **What:** Inter loads through `next/font/google` with its op
          +tical size axis (14 to 32). Headings set optical size 32, whic
          +h is Inter Display. No font files are self-hosted             
      423 +- **Why:** The build brief says to self-host only if `next/fon
          +t` can't load Inter Display. It can: Google's Inter is the var
          +iable version with both cuts in one file                      
      424 +- **Rejected:** Self-hosting Inter Display files. More files t
          +o license-check and serve, for the same result                
      425 +- **Revisit when:** Headings don't look like Inter Display on 
          +the preview                                                   
      426 +                                                              
      427 +## 2026-10-29 · Small controls are 44px, not Inspo Book 01's 4
          +2px                                                           
      428 +                                                              
      429 +- **What:** Small buttons, chips and list rows are 44px tall. 
          +Main buttons and segmented controls stay at Inspo Book 01's 54
          +px                                                            
      430 +- **Why:** The build brief sets tap targets at 44px or more, a
          +fter Apple's guidelines. Inspo Book 01's small size is 42px   
      431 +- **Rejected:** Keeping 42px with invisible padding around it.
          + Harder to keep consistent                                    
      432 +- **Revisit when:** Not expected to change                    
      433 +                                                              
      434 +## 2026-10-29 · Contrast-checked colours: #2F5FE0 for actions,
          + a darker grey for control borders                            
      435 +                                                              
      436 +- **What:** Buttons, links, selected states and the focus ring
          + use #2F5FE0 (5.5:1 with white), the example shade from `Desig
          +n fundamentals.md`. Borders of anything you tap use #7D879A (3
          +.6:1 on white) instead of Inspo Book 01's #DCE2EA (1.3:1). Err
          +ors use Inspo Book 01's #DC2626 (4.8:1), since Legion's public
          + palette has no red. Tailwind's default colours are switched o
          +ff. Ratios in `docs/design.md`                                
      437 +- **Why:** The brief requires 4.5:1 for text, and Apple's crit
          +eria ask for 3:1 for controls and selection states            
      438 +- **Rejected:** Legion's #3E70F7 for button fills (4.3:1, fail
          +s for normal text). Inspo's light border on controls (hard to 
          +see, fails 3:1)

⏺ PR 1 is open: https://github.com/Lily-Tewolde/legion-onboarding/pull/5. I've
  stopped there and haven't started PR 2.

  Preview: https://legion-onboarding-git-build-30108b-lilyatewolde-7305s-project
  s.vercel.app. It opens without a login, and the page has the banner and the
  help link.

  What's in it (5 commits on build/1-foundation):
  - Dark mode: the starter dark mode is gone.
  - Tokens: Legion's palette is in, with #2F5FE0 for buttons and links.
    Tailwind's default colours are switched off, so nothing off-palette can
    creep in. Spacing is Atlassian's 8px scale; radii, control heights, shadows
    and the focus ring come from Inspo Book 01.
  - Fonts: Inter comes from next/font with its optical-size axis. Inter Display
    is that axis at 32, so headings get it without any self-hosted font files.
    There's no Satoshi.
  - Components: Button, Chip, SegmentedControl, Switch, List and Tag. All use
    Legion blue, 16px text and tap targets of at least 44px.
  - Page shell: the demo banner, the "Talk to a person" link and help panel, and
    a progress bar. The shell's strings are in src/copy.ts, word for word from
    the copy draft.
  - Docs: docs/design.md covers the tokens with contrast ratios, the changes
    from Inspo and the shell. I added three entries to docs/decisions.md.

  How I checked it: lint, typecheck and build all pass. Locally, on a temporary
  page I deleted before committing, I checked:
  - the selected and error states
  - the switch
  - that the help panel keeps focus inside, and closes on Escape and on a tap
    outside
  - that heading fonts and 44px targets are right
  - that nothing scrolls sideways at 390px

  I clicked through locally, not on the preview. On the preview I only fetched
  the page.

  Two things you might not expect:
  - Inspo's small controls are 42px. I made them 44px to meet the brief's
    tap-target minimum.
  - Inspo's border grey was too faint on buttons and fields (1.3:1), so anything
    you tap has a darker border that passes 3:1.

  11 open questions are in the PR description. The ones that most need you:
  - Progress style: what it looks like, which screens show it and its
    screen-reader label are all undecided. I built a thin bar and haven't put it
    on any screen.
  - Switch or checkbox: the brief lists Switch, but the copy uses checkboxes for
    "earlier time", consent and "I'm safe". I built Switch as listed.
  - Missing copy: there's no browser tab title, favicon or header wordmark yet.

  I didn't update docs/work-log.md, because that log is kept by your other
  session.

⏺ Found it, and it isn't the network. This build has cacheComponents on, and
  Next 16 then keeps a page's React state when you navigate away and come back.
  Checking the docs to confirm:

  Ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Found the root cause: Next keeps recent pages cached with stale state, which
  leaves Continue disabled after "Go back" and lets old answers override "I'm
  safe." I'll fix this by syncing the form to an external store and resetting
  submission/checkbox state when pages are hidden.

⏺ Adding both rules. One subtlety first: a tap should only apply its own
  question's rule. If tapping Texas ran every rule, someone under 18 who had
  typed their date of birth would be screened out for age the moment they tapped
  "Yes", when the date of birth should wait for Continue. So taps get their own
  rule check, and the server is told which question was tapped, so what it
  saves always matches the screen shown.

⏺ PR 5 is open as #12:
  https://github.com/Lily-Tewolde/legion-onboarding/pull/12. The evals pass,
  GitHub shows no conflicts, and I've stopped there.

  What's in it:
  - "What's going on?" You can type, speak or tap the starter chips. The word
    list runs before any model sees the text, then the crisis flag and the AI
    step (Sonnet 5.5) run in parallel, and a crisis always wins. At most one
    clarifying question; mashed keys get one retry, then the simple form.
  - Slow or failed AI: "Still working on it…" at 5 seconds, and "Use a simple
    form instead" offered at 10, with the text kept. It only switches to the
    form on its own if the AI fails or a limit is hit.
  - The simple form uses Legion's conditions list and their three needs options.
  - The confirm screen: remove a chip, "+ Add", the Anxiety policy line, and
    "Have you been diagnosed with this?" when it's unknown. Your double-check
    rows use your wording: Texas, Medicaid or Medicare and the hospital question
    act on tap, and the date of birth is checked on "Looks right".
  - New screens: someone else, the 911-first immediate-danger screen, and the
    being-hurt screen with "Leave this page".
  - The limits: 30 calls per visitor per hour, plus the $10 daily cap. As you
    asked, the word list always runs, and the crisis flag still runs over a
    limit.

  Evals:
  - npm run eval passes with no failures. Every "What's going on?" bar is met,
    the crisis layers still pass at 5 runs per case, and the right crisis screen
    shows for every kind (20/20).
  - The grader check is 10/10, now that G-08 is scored a fail.
  - The evals caught two real prompt problems:
    - "My mood crashes" was labelled as Depression.
    - "Do I have bipolar?" was recorded as not diagnosed.

    The AI now only labels conditions the patient names or plainly describes,
    and never works out a diagnosis.

  Tested on the preview: every path, including the slow and failed AI (I
  simulated the network inside the page). My test visitor also went over 30
  calls in an hour, which confirmed the limit on the real preview: it opened the
  simple form while the crisis flag kept running. Supabase holds only standard
  labels and costs, never text.

  Bug found and fixed: after "Start over", the confirm screen bounced the next
  answer back to screen 2. Next had kept the old screen alive in the background,
  and its "nothing to confirm" redirect fired. Fixed and re-tested.

  Two things you might not expect:
  - Docs aren't edited, as with PR 10. The PR lists exactly what the copy draft
    and decision log need.
  - New specialties for the made-up providers: PTSD, OCD and Panic disorder are
    covered for every plan. Oscar + Sleep is still the only plan-and-condition
    pair with no times.

  One question in the PR: the 30-an-hour limit counts every AI call, the crisis
  flag included, so a visitor gets about 15 submits an hour. Should only the
  "What's going on?" call count?

      31  ## The problem and the data
      32  
      33  A Texas adult looking for psychiatric care has to get from "I n
          eed help" to a completed first visit. In Legion's last 30 days 
          of data (to Aug 29, 2026), 5,898 sessions start onboarding and 
          1,403 reach the payment page (23.8%). Nothing after the payment
           page is measured, so no one knows how many of them end in a fi
          rst visit, which is where onboarding ends.
